利率上行引發波動，惟無損 AI 供應鏈擴張動能
2026 年 10 月
9 月全球股市普遍回落，主因為油價急升、全球長端殖利率攀高及主要央行轉鷹。僅有日本股市
受 AI、半導體需求與企業信心支撐，抵銷 BOJ 升息壓力，成為主要市場中唯一正報酬。標普 500 9
月小跌 0.3%，雖 AI 與大型科技股獲利韌性仍在，但 Fed 於 9 月升息並暗示年底前再度升息，加上
油價與美債殖利率走高，壓抑估值並引發科技股震盪。印度股市下跌 6.9%最弱，主要受油價飆升、
全球利率上行及外資單月淨流出約 27 億美元拖累。中國股市下跌 4.7%，因受內需修復偏弱、科技

股評價承壓所致；歐洲股市也因油價單月大漲近 14%與全球殖利率攀升影響，9 月下跌 4.6%。
9 月主要債市全面下跌，核心壓力來自全球殖利率急升。美歐日央行同步升息、Brent 原油單月
上漲約 14%、 美國經濟數據偏強與公債供給疑慮 ， 使美國10 年期殖利率單月上升約 53bp，創 2022
年以來最大升幅。主要券種中，全球投資級公司債下跌 2.93%表現最弱，新興美元債亦下跌 2.91%，
因 9 月全球公債殖利率快速攀升，此兩類債券對利率變化均較敏感 ； 其中投資級公司債相較高收益債
存續期較長、票息緩衝較低，因此在利率重新定價期間承受較大價格壓力。亞洲高收益債下挫 1.20%
相對抗跌，因高票息與較短存續期提供緩衝；新興當地債跌 1.74%，亦優於多數美元債種。
在股市方面，AI 基礎建設需求持續擴張，帶動 HBM、伺服器 DRAM、先進製程與封裝等關鍵供
應鏈維持成長動能，市場表現由全面上漲逐步轉向結構性分化。若AI 伺服器需求持續成長且 CSP 資
本支出維持高檔，具供給優勢、定價能力及較高訂單能見度之企業，其營運表現有機會獲得支撐。整

體而言 ， 科技產業與台灣及日本股市獲利表現仍具關注度 ， 惟後續仍須留意全球景氣 、 產業供需變化 、
政策監管及市場評價調整等風險因素。
就債市而言，雖然貨幣政策風險下降有助於穩定長期通膨預期，但政府與企業發債供給增加所帶
來的排擠效應，以及美國經濟成長展現韌性，仍可能使長天期利率面臨上行壓力。因此，在當前利率
環境下，中天期債券於票息收益與存續期間風險之間相對較為均衡。投資級債方面，彭博美國投資級
公司債指數利差約 80 個基點 ， 雖然利差偏窄 ， 但高基準利率環境仍提供相對較高的收益水準 。 因此，
在目前市場環境下 ， 中天期美國公債及投資級債仍為值得持續觀察的債券資產類別 ， 惟後續表現仍須
留意利率走勢、信用利差變化及經濟成長動能等因素。

美國股市：通膨與升息預期上升推動美債利率上漲，美股呈高檔區間震盪
9 月因美伊關係緊張升溫 ， 推升油價並加劇市場對通膨回升的擔憂 ；相較於 6 月點陣圖 ，9 月FOMC
官員全面上修 2026 至 2027 年的政策利率預估至 4.1%，並暗示 2026 年底前會再升息一次，加上美
國經濟數據表現優於預期，使美國公債殖利率走升，進而壓抑美股表現。在此背景下，9 月標普 500
指數呈現高檔區間震盪格局。產業表現方面，資訊科技與通訊服務類股相對強勢，持續扮演指數上漲
的主要動能；相較之下，醫療保健、必需消費及部分工業類股表現較為疲弱。

9 月美國聯準會貨幣政策會議以 12 比 0 的表決結果 ， 將聯邦基金利率目標區間上調25 個基點至
3.75%到 4.00%。9 月最新經濟預測，美國聯準會官員上修 2026 年核心 PCE 至 3.4%，2027 年維持
在 2.5%，將 2026-2027 年經濟成長上調 0.1%至 2.3%與 2.4%，顯示聯準會認為美國經濟與通膨仍
具韌性。
觀察美國近期的經濟數據可以發現企業投資動能依然強健，元大投顧表示，美國8 月扣除飛機與
國防的核心資本財訂單由前期的月增 0.6%加速至 1.6%， 其中初級金屬 、 機械及電機設備訂單均呈現
增長，顯示 AI 相關投資及企業設備更新需求持續支撐資本財訂單，企業設備投資可望對經濟成長形
成穩健貢獻。
自 6 月底以來 ， 市場持續上修標普500 指數企業獲利預估 ， 其中以科技與能源類股貢獻最為明顯 ，
反映企業營運表現優於市場預期。AI 投資循環持續推進、企業數位轉型需求增加，以及美國內需維
持穩健，共同支撐企業獲利成長動能，並有助於吸收高利率環境帶來的壓力。與此同時，標普 500

指數前瞻本益比已由先前高點回落，評價壓力較先前明顯緩解。隨著企業獲利持續改善，美股行情正
由過去仰賴資金與情緒推升評價 ， 逐步轉向由基本面支撐股價 ， 後續表現將更取決於企業獲利是否持
續優於預期。
投資策略方面，投資焦點已逐漸由評價擴張轉向企業獲利成長與基本面表現。短期市場仍須留意
政策面不確定性 ， 美中貿易談判進展將牽動全球供應鏈與科技產業展望 ， 若關稅或科技限制措施再度
升溫，可能對科技股評價造成壓力；此外，美國期中選舉逐漸接近，企業稅率、科技監管及產業政策
等議題亦可能影響市場風險偏好。整體而言，企業獲利改善仍是支撐美股最重要的力量，若 AI 投資
循環與獲利成長趨勢延續，美股中長期表現仍可望由基本面驅動。

歐洲股市：儘管企業獲利上修，仍需持續關注地緣政治發展
9 月泛歐 600 指數下跌約 2%，產業表現方面 ，製藥 、生技、銀行及部分科技類股表現相對強勢，
顯示資金偏好具獲利能見度與防禦特性的族群 ； 相較之下 ， 礦業 、 消費及部分金融服務類股表現落後 ，
反映市場對歐洲經濟成長及企業獲利前景仍持審慎態度。整體而言，受到高油價以及高利率環境，投
資氣氛轉趨保守，市場資金持續聚焦於基本面較穩健的產業。
歐洲央行 9 月政策利率由 2.25% 升息 1 碼至 2.50 % ，符合市場預期。歐洲央行最新經濟預
測中上修中期通膨展望，2027 年消費者物價指數年增率由 2.3% 升至 2.5% ，2028 年則由 2.0%

上調至 2.1% ；核心消費者物價指數方面，2027 年和 2028 年預測亦各上修 0.1 % 至 2.6% 及
2.3% ，能源成本上升對食品及其他價格的傳導，使得通膨回落速度較原先預期緩慢。ECB 總裁拉加
德表示能源價格上漲逐步傳導至核心及食品通膨，預期整體通膨在 2027 年上半年維持在高檔。
根據花旗歐洲獲利修正指數(Earnings Revision Index) ，歐洲企業獲利預估已連續上修並到近五
年高點，反映企業基本面持續改善。受惠於歐元區景氣穩步復甦、製造業景氣回升，以及企業資本支
出逐步回暖，工業、金融及景氣循環類股獲利展望同步改善。隨著市場對企業未來盈餘預期提高，有
助支撐歐股評價面與資金流入，並成為推動泛歐 600 指數表現的重要動能。
然而，自 3 月美伊衝突爆發以來，市場對能源供給中斷的擔憂持續升溫，帶動油價走高，進而增
加仰賴進口能源的歐洲企業成本壓力，並壓抑歐股相對表現。進入9 月後，俄羅斯與北約緊張關係再
度升溫，歐洲安全局勢不確定性提高，進一步加劇市場對能源供應與地緣政治風險的擔憂。若相關衝

突持續延燒，不僅可能推升能源價格與通膨壓力，也可能影響企業投資意願及市場風險偏好，進而對
歐洲企業獲利與股市表現形成壓力。
展望 10 月，歐股仍將受到高油價、地緣政治風險及歐洲央行維持偏鷹立場等因素影響，市場波
動性可能維持偏高。近期能源價格攀升推升通膨預期，使 ECB 傾向維持較高利率水準，不僅提高企
業融資成本，也限制股市評價進一步擴張的空間。此外，中東局勢持續緊張，以及俄羅斯與北約間的
緊張關係再度升高，均可能影響投資人風險偏好，增添歐洲金融市場不確定性。雖然歐元區經濟活動
仍維持溫和擴張，企業獲利預估亦持續改善，但在高利率與地緣政治風險尚未明顯緩解之前，市場情
緒仍偏向審慎，後續仍需持續關注能源價格、通膨走勢及地緣政治發展，作為判斷歐股能否重啟上漲
動能的重要觀察指標。

日本股市：升息落地，景氣展望佳，第四季半導體與商社企業獲利動能有望延續
9 月日本股市受升息預期及長天期公債殖利率走高影響，部分高評價股票出現震盪，但這波調整
較偏向政策轉折下的評價重估，而非企業基本面全面轉弱。日本銀行於 9 月 18 日決議將短期政策利
率目標提高至約 1.25%，使市場反覆討論的升息方向逐漸明朗。隨著政策落地，投資焦點有望由利率
變動轉回企業營運與獲利。從正面角度看，利率正常化反映日本經濟具備一定韌性，股價整理也有助
於消化先前累積的評價壓力。若後續企業財測保持穩健，9 月的震盪可望成為第四季行情重新蓄勢的

過程。
日本股市的中期支撐，來自薪資成長、內需改善與企業投資的相互帶動。2026 年春鬥最終統計
顯示，5,368 個工會的平均調薪幅度達 5.01%，連續第三年維持在 5%以上；可明確辨識的基本薪資
調升幅度亦達 3.50%。薪資持續增加，有助於提升家庭消費能力，為零售與服務等內需產業提供較穩
定的需求基礎。若企業能透過產品競爭力、定價能力及經營效率吸收成本，消費回溫便有機會轉化為
獲利成長。同時，全球 AI 相關投資也為日本半導體設備、載板及電子零組件供應鏈創造機會，使內
需復甦與科技需求形成雙重支撐。
展望 10 月，隨著升息議題逐步由預期轉為已知條件，市場可望更加重視第四季訂單、企業財測
及實際獲利。科技產業方面，若全球 AI 相關資本支出持續擴大，日本具技術優勢的設備與零組件企
業，有機會將需求轉化為接單及出貨成長；商社與資源相關企業，則可觀察能源、原物料業務及投資
收益對現金流的貢獻。內需產業亦值得留意薪資成長能否帶動消費，以及企業能否維持利潤率。整體
而言，10 月的投資機會不必侷限於單一題材，市場有望逐步從政策消息主導，轉向由產業需求與企

業業績支撐的行情。
投資策略方面，半導體供應鏈可優先挑選訂單能見度高、具技術門檻且獲利成長明確的企業；商
社則著重現金流、資本配置與股東回饋，內需股則觀察消費回溫能否反映在營收及利潤。若股價因利
率或匯率波動而短暫回落，但企業基本面與財測未明顯改變，可考慮分批布局，避免追逐短線漲幅。
後續仍須追蹤長天期利率 、 日圓及企業財測變化 ， 但投資主軸可更積極地放在具備實質成長能力的公
司。日本央行升息落地後，企業獲利能否接棒，將是日本股市延續表現的關鍵。

台灣股市：訂單持續暢旺突破千億，AI 需求持續向供應鏈擴散，廠商展望樂觀
9 月觀察台股基本面，最明確的支撐來自 8 月外銷訂單創下單月新高，以及企業對 9 月接單維持
擴張預期。經濟部公布，8 月外銷訂單達 1,029.6 億美元，月增 5.1%、年增 71.4%；其中資訊通信
與電子產品合計 799.6 億美元，占整體 77.7%，顯示 AI 伺服器、半導體與雲端建設仍是主要成長來
源。受查廠商對 9 月接單的預期，以訂單金額計算的動向指數為 52.1，高於代表持平的 50，意味整
體接單有望較 8 月增加。雖然 9 月實際結果仍待公布，但已發布的數據與企業展望，為台股進入第

四季提供了正面的基本面依據。
更值得留意的是，AI 需求正從核心晶片延伸至更廣的供應鏈。雲端業者擴充運算能力，除了帶動
伺服器與半導體接單，也使自研 ASIC、IC 設計及相關電子零組件成為後續觀察重點；半導體投資與
設備擴產 ， 則為自動化設備 、 銅箔及高階材料創造機會 。8 月機械與基本金屬外銷訂單分別年增 28.3%
及 26.8%，顯示科技投資的效益已反映在部分非電子貨品。對台股而言，這有助於投資主線從少數大
型權值股，延伸至具技術優勢與訂單能見度的中上游企業。不過，面板需求仍偏弱，產業表現並非全
面同步，選股仍須辨別企業實際受惠程度。
展望 10 月，市場焦點可望轉向第三季財報、第四季營運展望，以及 9 月外銷訂單能否驗證接單
續強。就廠商對 9 月的預期而言，資訊通信與電子產品的訂單金額動向指數分別為 52.4 及 51.5，顯
示科技需求仍具支撐；基本金屬與化學品則達 59.0 及 61.0，呈現部分材料領域接單改善的期待。若
AI 伺服器出貨 、 自研晶片開發及半導體資本支出持續推進 ， 相關台灣供應鏈的營收與獲利有望受惠。

另一方面，機械與光學器材指數分別為 46.3 及 49.5，提醒市場不能將整體訂單成長視為所有產業同
步復甦，仍須以後續實績檢驗。
投資策略方面，10 月以「掌握 AI 主線、尋找擴散機會、重視獲利驗證」作為配置方向。核心部
位可留意伺服器、半導體及 IC 設計等需求較明確的企業，並觀察 ASIC 相關業務能否由開發逐步轉
化為穩定出貨；其他機會則可從設備、銅箔與高階材料中，篩選確實取得訂單的公司。選股不只看產
業接單增幅，更應比較個別企業的營收成長、毛利率與獲利展望，避免在評價已高時追逐短期題材。
整體而言，台股仍有基本面支撐，後續行情能否擴大，關鍵在於 AI 需求能否持續轉化為更多企業的
實際獲利。

中國股市：AI 產業規模擴張難掩獲利困境，產業進入變現考驗
9 月中國 AI 產業的討論，從模型能力與使用量，逐漸轉向更根本的問題：在高階晶片取得受限、
業者競爭激烈的環境下，龐大的投入能否形成可持續的回報。9 月中美高層會談後，雙方宣布建立
AI 相關對話機制，但公開成果未顯示高階 AI 晶片出口限制已放寬。政策溝通有所進展，並未立即改
變企業取得先進算力的條件 。 與此同時 ， 低價及開放權重策略持續降低AI 使用門檻 ， 帶動市場擴張，
也使企業更難單靠模型服務取得理想利潤。對投資人而言，9 月最重要的訊號不是單一產品的表現，

而是中國 AI 已進入需要同時回答算力效率、商業變現與資本回收的階段。
從整體結構來看，中國具備電力、製造基礎及廣泛應用場景，有利於 AI 以較低成本進入企業營
運與日常服務；但部署成本較低，不代表產業獲利便能同步提高。當業者以低價爭取用戶，使用量可
以迅速累積，收入卻可能跟不上基礎設施、研發及營運支出。以特定平台的觀察為例，DeepSeek 曾
取得可觀的代幣使用量，但相應支出占比明顯較低；這不能代表中國 AI 產業的整體財務表現，卻凸
顯「有人使用」與「願意付費」之間仍有距離。未來產業能否改善獲利，取決於企業是否找到具持續
需求的應用、建立合理收費方式，並避免同質化服務陷入反覆降價。
展望 10 月 ， 中國AI 的成長空間仍在 ， 但市場衡量進展的標準應由使用規模 ， 逐步轉向投入產出。
若 AI 更深入企業工作流程，相關需求有望延續；然而，需求增加後，業者仍須證明服務品質、運算
效率與客戶付費能同步改善 。 晶片限制使算力使用效率更加重要 ， 價格競爭則使收入品質成為另一項
考驗。Vercel 資料顯示，8 月開放權重模型在其平台占代幣使用量 56%，支出占比卻為 14%，說明

高使用量未必對應高支出；10 月宜觀察企業是否開始披露更清楚的付費需求與成本改善，而非只比
較流量或模型排名。
投資策略方面，對中國 AI 宜維持關注成長、審慎評估獲利的態度，避免把產業滲透率提高，直
接等同於所有企業的投資價值提升。評估重點可放在三個整體問題：第一，持續增加的算力與研發投
入，能否換來足夠的收入；第二，低價競爭下，企業能否留住付費客戶並維持合理利潤；第三，AI
應用能否從試用走向長期採購。具備穩定需求、成本控制能力及清楚收費模式的企業，較有機會在產
業整合中建立優勢。整體而言，中國 AI 並非缺少使用場景，而是仍須證明大規模使用可以轉化為可
持續的獲利，這將是 10 月觀察產業與相關市場表現的核心。

科技類股：AI 應用加速落地，光通訊與 Agent 入口迎來成長機會
AI 產業的成長主軸正由模型訓練延伸至推論與實際應用 ， 讓軟體服務與硬體建設形成相互帶動的
循環。當 AI 不再只回答問題，而能協助搜尋資訊、安排活動及完成跨平台任務，使用頻率與運算需
求有望提高。Meta 於 9 月推出個人 AI 助理 Muse，透過應用程式及 WhatsApp 接觸既有用戶，積
極爭取 AI 服務入口。對產業而言，Agent 普及不僅意味新的服務形式，也可能擴大雲端運算與資料
傳輸需求，使應用端的創新成為基礎設施升級的推力。投資機會可從使用者入口延伸至相關供應鏈。

在硬體端，AI 推論與多步驟任務若持續增加，資料中心內部的資料交換將更頻繁，高速且節能的
連接能力因而更具價值 。 光通訊除支援資料中心之間的傳輸 ， 也逐步朝機櫃及晶片附近的互連場景發
展 ， 拓展供應商的產品空間 。Lumentum 指出 ，AI 運算對速度與頻寬的要求提升 ， 帶動光連結需求，
並已在營收展望中反映相關成長 。 高速光模組與新型互連方案也持續推進 。 雖然部分新技術仍須經過
客戶導入與量產驗證，但既有產品需求與後續技術升級有望接續，讓光通訊成為 AI 基礎設施中值得
持續關注的環節。
在應用端，Muse 代表 AI 從被動回覆走向主動協助執行任務的嘗試。若 Agent 能替使用者完成
搜尋、比較、預訂與付款等流程，便利性提升有望促進使用黏著度，並為支付、交易與服務串接平台
創造新的合作機會。跨平台操作可能由單一入口整合，促使各產業重新思考客戶服務。 「Muse 對各
產業的影響」圖可呈現商業模式轉變的可能方向。隨著更多服務接入 Agent 生態系，使用情境有望
持續擴展；後續若任務完成率、使用留存及付費轉換逐步改善，應用端便有機會形成更明確的收入來

源，並進一步支持運算與傳輸投資。
投資策略方面，建議以「應用入口與基礎設施並重」掌握 AI 從訓練走向推論的成長機會。硬體
方面，可優先關注已切入主要客戶供應鏈、具備高速光模組與新型互連產品布局，且訂單能見度較佳
的業者。應用方面，則可追蹤具備用戶基礎、服務整合能力與清楚收費模式的平台，觀察 Agent 能
否從新產品熱度轉化為持續使用與付費需求 。 配置上可先以業績較明確的基礎設施業者為核心 ， 再逐
步增加應用平台的布局，兼顧當前需求與後續成長空間。整體而言，AI 應用愈深入日常生活與企業
流程 ， 入口平台與光通訊供應鏈愈有機會相互受惠 ； 選股關鍵仍是找出能將需求成長轉化為實際業績
的企業。

拉美市場：10 月巴西總統大選將登場，政治不確定性影響市場表現
9 月拉美市場維持震盪整理 。 一方面 ， 拉美各國通膨數據普遍低於市場預期 ，有利市場降息預期；
另一方面，巴西總統大選選情持續拉鋸，政治不確定性升溫則讓投資人保持觀望。展望 10 月，隨巴
西大選投票日逼近，政治因素仍可能使拉美市場短線波動加劇。
受到美伊衝突帶動油價上漲影響，市場原本擔心燃料與運輸成本增加，會讓拉美物價再度大幅上
漲，進而迫使拉美央行升息。然而，高盛分析指出，拉美市場目前的通膨率仍受到控制，當地經濟承
受能源漲價的能力 ， 比市場原先擔心的情況更好 。 其中一個重要原因是 ， 新興市場政府透過燃油補貼 、

稅制安排與零售價格管制，讓消費者實際感受到的價格漲幅相對有限。此外，上半年全球農產品供給
較為充裕，使食品價格漲幅偏低，也抵銷了部分能源漲價帶來的影響。由於拉美市場實際通膨壓力小
於原先市場預期，央行進一步大幅升息的必要性也隨之降低，高盛因此認為，市場目前對多數拉美經
濟體未來一至兩年的升息預期過於悲觀，並預期拉美各國央行在 2027 年較可能重新轉向降息。
不過，拉美多國經濟出現放緩跡象，亦可能壓抑股市上漲空間。智利央行於 9 月會議維持政策利
率 4.50%不變 ， 並將2026 年經濟成長預測下修至 0.25%至 0.75%， 低於6 月預估的 1.0%至 1.75%。
智利央行指出，第二季內需成長放緩，勞動市場及信心指標同步轉弱，加上燃料價格上漲侵蝕企業與
家庭所得，均對經濟活動形成壓力。巴西經濟亦出現放緩跡象，巴西央行於 9 月 16 日一致決議調降
Selic 政策利率 25 個基點至 13.75%，並指出景氣循環產業動能轉弱，且整體與核心通膨均已回落至
目標區間上限以下，為進一步降息提供空間。不過，巴西央行並未承諾下次會議是否將繼續降息，而

是強調將視未來經濟與通膨數據逐次評估。
另一方面，巴西總統大選進入白熱化階段則是當前影響市場最主要的因子。市場民調顯示，相較
7 月時現任總統魯拉在民調中普遍領先競爭者波索納洛約 5 至 8 個百分點 ，在 9 月的民調雙方差距已
縮小至 2 個百分點以內，選情不確定性仍高。若選舉最終由魯拉勝選，其支持財政擴張的政策，將可
能使市場對於巴西財政赤字問題的擔憂加劇，進而使巴西股市面臨較大壓力；而若波索納洛勝選，市
場普遍預期其能提出改善投資人信心的財政政策 ， 為巴西央行創造更多降息空間 ， 有利後續巴西股市
上行。
整體而言，儘管市場對拉美 各國當前升息預期可能過於悲觀，但目前拉美多國經濟成長同步放
緩，加上巴西總統大選選情膠著，選後財政政策方向不確定性高，在考量大選及政策方向不確定性的
狀況下，短線拉美市場波動恐將加劇。

能源類股：全球石油庫存持續下降，但中東石油出口已出現回升
9 月西德州原油期貨價格受美伊衝突升溫影響，一度於月中重新站上每桶 100 美元，其後隨衝突
趨緩，油價漲幅逐步收斂，月底約回落至90 美元附近，能源類股表現亦大致跟隨油價走勢。展望10
月，近期中東石油供應恢復速度不如預期，加上庫存持續下降，預期油價下檔仍具支撐。然而，市場
數據顯示，高油價及供應受阻已同步抑制石油需求，且中東各國原油出口持續回升，後續供應緊張情
況有望逐步改善。因此，雖然中東地緣政治風險仍將對油價形成支撐，但隨美伊重啟外交接觸，油價
進一步大幅上漲的空間預期相對有限。

受到美伊談判僵持影響，國際能源署（IEA）與美國能源資訊署（EIA）在 9 月月報均將中東石油
供應恢復的預期時間延後。IEA 將中東石油供應全面恢復的預期時間延至 2027 年，並將 2026 年全
球石油供應減幅，由上月預估的每日減少 430 萬桶擴大至 570 萬桶；EIA 則將中東石油供給恢復至
衝突前水準的時間，由 2027 年初延至第 2 季。
此外 ，受到中東供應受阻與油價偏高影響 ，IEA 亦將 2026 年全球石油需求預測下修每日 94 萬桶 ，
預估全年需求將較去年減少每日 250 萬桶，EIA 則預估全年石油需求將減少約每日 170 萬桶，石油
輸出國組織（OPEC）也將今年需求由上月預估的每日增加 60 萬桶下修至 40 萬桶。
受此影響，全球石油庫存持續下降。IEA 估計，8 月全球可觀察石油庫存減少 9,500 萬桶，自 2
月底以來已累計下降 5.07 億桶，顯示市場仍持續動用庫存填補缺口。EIA 也預期全球石油庫存將持
續下降至年底，因此將2026 年下半年布蘭特原油均價預測，由上月約每桶82 美元上調至 90 美元；

此外 ，隨著 2027 年中東出口與產量逐步恢復 ，IEA 預估明年全年布蘭特原油均價將回落至 74 美元。
另一方面，中東原油出口則已經出現回升跡象。路透社 9 月 28 日引述 Kpler 初步資料指出，沙
烏地阿拉伯與阿聯酋 9 月原油出口均出現上升 ， 帶動中東主要產油國當月原油日均出口量回升至每日
1,280 萬桶，創下 2 月戰爭爆發以來最高水準。其中，沙烏地阿拉伯9 月原油日均出口預估將達每日
540 萬桶，較 8 月的 244.6 萬桶增加逾一倍。由於沙國東西向輸油管線先前因攻擊受損，沙烏地計
畫將部分原先經紅海延布港出口的原油 ， 轉往波斯灣沿岸港口裝運 ， 帶動拉斯坦努拉港9 月日均出貨
量預估由 8 月的每日 92.9 萬桶增至 360 萬桶。
整體而言，中東供應恢復時程延後，加上全球石油庫存持續下降，短期仍有利支撐油價。然而，
石油需求預估同步下修 ， 且沙烏地阿拉伯與阿聯酋原油出口均已逐步回升 ，後續石油供應緊張程度可
望有所緩解。整體而言，儘管油價仍受中東地緣風險支撐，但隨美國期中選舉接近，近期美伊亦重啟

外交接觸，市場對原油供應中斷的擔憂可望逐步降溫，油價進一步大幅上漲空間相對有限。

黃金類股：升息預期攀升壓抑金價表現；財富配置及央行買盤支撐中長期需求
近期黃金價格表現較為疲弱，主要受到市場升息預期持續攀升，帶動美債殖利率走高，增加持有
黃金的機會成本，對於利率較為敏感的黃金 ETF 需求因此受到壓抑。然而，高盛研究指出，聯準會
近期決議升息雖然延長了市場高利率環境時間，但並不會進一步墊高利率終點 ， 加上全球央行持續購
金，以及投資人從財富配置角度增加黃金配置，均有助於支撐中期黃金需求。
高盛報告指出，聯準會於 9 月升息一碼，增加持有黃金的機會成本，使對利率敏感的黃金ETF 需
求下滑 ， 高盛因此將2026 年底金價預估由每盎司 4,900 美元下修至 4,650 美元 。 然而 ， 高盛也預估，

聯準會將於 2027 年 9 月至 2028 年 3 月期間降息三碼，使政策利率最終回到 3.25%至 3.5%區間，
故儘管聯準會 9 月升息延長了高利率環境，但實際並未墊高利率終點，因此來自黃金 ETF 的賣壓僅
會延後金價上漲時程，但並未改變高盛對黃金的偏多看法。
另一方面，央行買盤仍是金價支撐核心。高盛9 月報告預估，全球央行近三個月經季節調整後的
購金速度已上升至每月 91 噸，遠高於 2022 年前每月 17 噸的平均水準。高盛指出，這股需求主要
來自分散外匯儲備的考量，在 2022 年俄羅斯央行資產遭美國凍結後，各國央行更加重視儲備資產遭
制裁或凍結的風險，相較於對利率敏感的 ETF 資金，央行基於儲備分散而進行的購金，不易因短期
利率變動而轉向，因此即使聯準會升息，央行買盤仍有利支撐金價表現。
除了央行買盤外，花旗也從全球財富配置的角度看好黃金。花旗指出，全球每年新增黃金礦產供
應規模相當有限，若全球家庭額外將 0.1%的財富配置於黃金，就相當於約 4,459 噸黃金購買量，也

等於 1.2 年的新增礦產供應量。換言之，即使全球投資人僅小幅提高黃金配置比重，也可能創造遠超
過新增供給的需求。由於黃金供給短期內難以快速增加，市場往往需要透過金價上漲，吸引既有持有
人釋出黃金以滿足新增需求，因此資產配置需求增加可能對金價產生顯著推升影響。
不過，花旗也指出，黃金新增供給有限的特性雖有助於放大需求對金價的推升效果，但同時也可
能提高金價出現較劇烈修正的風險。花旗估計，2022 年以來的金價上漲已使既有黃金持有人的帳面
財富增加約 13 兆美元，若持有人將其中約 1 兆美元轉回股票、債券或現金，所形成的黃金賣壓便可
能超過目前全部實體黃金需求，故當持有人集中獲利了結時，金價也可能出現較劇烈的修正。
整體而言，在市場升息預期持續攀升的情況下，短期黃金ETF 需求可能受到壓抑，金價仍可能面
臨震盪 。 然而 ， 全球央行基於外匯儲備分散需求持續購金 ， 加上投資人從財富配置角度提高黃金配置 ，
均有助於支撐中期黃金需求。

固定收益：聯準會升息穩定通膨預期，凸顯中天期美債與投資級債吸引力
美國 9 月聯準會貨幣政策會議以 12 比 0 全票通過 ， 將聯邦基金利率目標區間上調 25 個基點至
3.75%–4.00%。最新經濟預測同步將 2026 年核心 PCE 上修至 3.4%、GDP 成長率上修至 2.3%，且
全面上修 2026 至 2027 年政策利率預估至 4.1%，並暗示年底前仍有一次升息空間。9 月聯準會採取
預防性升息，主要是在避免通膨預期鬆動並維持政策公信力，確立高利率維持更久的政策基調。
美國政府於 9 月 30 日公布第二季國內生產毛額(GDP)終值，經季節調整後成長年率由 1.5%上修

至 2.2%，高於經濟學家預估的維持不變。第一季 GDP 季增年率也由 2.1%上修至 2.5%。其中，占
美國經濟活動逾 6 成的消費支出，第二季成長率由 3.4%上修至 3.8%，顯示美國消費動能強勁；元
大投顧指出，美國 8 月核心資本財訂單月增率加速至 1.6%，其中初級金屬、機械及電機設備訂單均
呈現增長，顯示 AI 相關投資及企業設備更新需求持續支撐資本財訂單，企業設備投資可望對經濟成
長形成穩健貢獻。在消費與企業動能表現強勁下，使市場重新評估美國經濟對通膨黏性的影響。
彭博 10 月 1 日的報告指出，在 Market Pulse 調查的 173 名受訪者，超過一半的人預測美國 30
年期公債殖利率在年底將達到 6%。儘管伊朗戰爭和高借貸成本持續影響 ， 美國經濟仍維持強勁成長 ，
這種韌性加劇了通膨和進一步升息的⾵險；人工智慧基礎設施建設競賽引發了企業發債的狂潮 ，推升
長天期債券發行量，全球企業今年以來已發行超過 4000 億美元的債券，用於技術投資，而其中大部
分債券發行於美國。另外，各國政府持續的預算赤字，美國聯邦政府債務規模首次突破 40 兆美元，

美國政府需要擴大發債量 ， 投資人要求更高的利率來購買債券等因素 ，皆為推升美國長天期殖利率上
行的原因。因此，長天期債券後續表現仍須持續觀察利率走勢、債券供需變化及通膨發展。
展望 10 月，由於美國通膨仍高於聯準會 2%的目標，2026 年底有再升息一碼的可能，使得短
天期利率下行空間有限。在政策利率可能維持高檔、長天期殖利率又受到供給面壓力推升的環境下，
當前的債券配置應以「息收防禦」與「存續期間控管」為核心。隨著美國公債利率上行，彭博美國投
資級債指數平均剩餘到期期限持續下降 ， 存續期間縮短有助降低利率變動對債券價格的影響 。美國債
市投資建議重點配置中天期美國公債與投資級公司債 。中天期美國公債與投資級公司債不僅能鎖定具
吸引力的收益率水準，也能兼顧存續期間風險控管，降低利率波動對投資組合淨值的衝擊，在當前環
境下有助於兼顧收益與風險控制。

Investment Thesis & PT methodology and risks

Investment Thesis - Unimicron Technology 
Unimicron is a key supplier of ABF substrates (25% market share in 2025) and BT 
substrates and also produces FPC/HDI/PCB products. We note (1) over 70% of ABF 
shipments are covered by long-term agreements (LTA), which limits pricing flexibility and 
makes price hikes less likely; (2) the company’s execution track record has been weaker 
than peers; (3) capacity expansion plan remains slower than peers, constraining its 
ability to capture incremental AI demand. The current ABF substrates market is seeing 
more favorable supply demand outlook, and Unimicron is likely to see a better pricing 
outlook to reflect rising material costs in the coming quarters. AI ABF accounts for 10% 
of the company’s total revenue, but the growth is likely to be slower than peers as the 
company is losing market share given its less favourable production yield and slow 
capacity expansion. Overall, we are Neutral rated on Unimicron given our outlook for 
limited upside potential, barring ASIC AI servers, despite our generally positive view in 
the long term on its profitability trajectory. 
Price Target Risks and Methodology - Unimicron Technology 
Valuation methodology: Our 12-month target price of NT$1,270 for Unimicron is 
based on 17.4x 2028E P/E, which is 2.7x STDV higher than the past upcycle average P/E 
(10.3x).  
Key upside/downside risks: (1) Slower/faster-than-expected PC demand recovery, (2) 
Slower/faster-than-expected ABF substrates upgrading pace, and (3) Slower/faster 
than-expected AI server PCB market share loss progress.

Hon Precision (7769.TW)
28 October 2026 Citi Research
6
Hon Precision
Company description
Hon Precision is a leading Taiwanese manufacturer specializing in IC
semiconductor automation equipment. Headquartered in Taichung City,
Taiwan, it is most known globally for providing high-precision IC test
handlers, temperature control solutions, and complete automated integrated
solutions for major OSAT and IDM customers.
Investment strategy
We have a Buy rating on Hon Precision as we view it sitting in a structurally

protected position at the heart of the AI/HPC back-end test cycle, with
growth visibility extending well into the next few years — the company holds
>90% share of the AI/HPC FT handler market and 60-70% of the ASIC SLT
market. We forecast a solid strong sales revenue CAGR in 2025-28E with
gross margin continuing to expand. In our view, the binding constraint on Hon
Precision's near-term trajectory is now capacity, rather than demand —
placing the company in the early phase of a multi-year structural growth and

margin-expansion cycle.
Valuation
Our target price of NT$10,000 is based on a target PER multiple of 31x applied
to the average of our 2027E/28E EPS estimates. Our PER target of 31x is set at
0.5-std above its 1-year PER average, justified by its high share of the AI/HPC
testing handler market, which we estimate could support an earnings CAGR
of > 75% in 2025-28, higher than its 3-year CAGR at c40% in 2022-25. Our
estimated > 75% earning CAGR is also higher than the +40% average

(Bloomberg consensus) of its global testing equipment peers. As its peers
have been trading at  average  35x PER on 2027 /28 consensus EPS in the past
1 year, we believe Hon Precision deserves a further re-rating to our target
PER.
Risks
Our quantitative risk rating system, which looks at historical share price
volatility, rates Hon Precision High Risk. However, we are not assigning a High
Risk rating as we forecast an earnings CAGR of > 75%, significantly stronger

than the growth outlook for its global peers. Also, we note its dominant
position in AI/HPC handler market and expanding market TAM opportunity,
which support our constructive view over its growth outlook.
Key downside risks that could impede the shares from reaching our target
price: 1) Global AI demand slowdown  driving weaker capacity build for
semiconductor equipment; 2) unexpected share losses at its major AI
projects; 3) slower-than-expected capacity ramp at its new facilities, or the

company taking a long time to find/secure new sites; 4) slower-than-
expected development of its CPO business; 5) shorter-than-expected testing
time leading to lower demand for its products; 6) weak adoption of high-

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.

reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds

### Page 3

Hon Hai (2317.TW; Buy, on CL): New AI server racks and Foldable phones to drive 4Q26E

We expect Hon Hai’s monthly revenues to continue sequential growth through Dec 
2026, leading 4Q26 revenues to grow at +39% QoQ, driven by: (1) AI data center: The 
next-generation AI server racks and CPO switches ramp-up, with higher ASP and early 
delivery. We expect Hon Hai to be the first to deliver the latest model, around 2-3 
months ahead of peers, driving its Oct and onward revenue growth. Followed by 
GPU-powered AI server racks, the LPU-powered AI server racks should fuel the growth 
in Dec and onward, supporting the YoY increase in 1Q27. As our Global Server TAM 
update shows, we expect the AI server rack global value TAM to grow at 163% CAGR in 
2025-28E to US$1tn, or 63% of the global server value TAM by 2028E. And (2) 
Smartphone: riding on the major brand customer’s latest model launches along with the 
new form factor to attract more end users. As we highlighted in September (report link), 
we reduced our global smartphone volume estimates by 1m/ 8m/ 0.2m units to 1.1bn / 
1.2bn/ 1.2bn in 2026-28E, or -10% / +2%/ +2% YoY on high memory costs; however, we 
remain positive on the potential for global leading brands to outperform the market 
given their strong brand awareness, large procurement power, and customers’ stronger 
consumption power. We remain positive on foldable phones and expect premium 
models to continue to gain market share, which should be positive to Hon Hai. Maintain 
Buy (on CL).

3-month revenues preview: We expect the company’s 4Q26E revenues to be +61% 
YoY/ +39% QoQ to NT$4.2 trillion. 3Q26 delivered strong revenues YoY growth of 47%, 
or 5% / 7% ahead of our / Bloomberg consensus, and we expect to see both YoY and 
MoM growth throughout Oct-Dec, driven by new AI server racks, new smartphone 
models, and CPO switches. Management noted that AI-related products remain on track 
to maintain solid growth momentum through 4Q26, while ICT products continue to 
benefit from typical 2H seasonal tailwinds, which should support overall operational 
performance in line with current consensus expectations.

### Page 4

Price Target Risks and Methodology – Hon Hai 
Valuation methodology: We are Buy rated on Hon Hai. Our 12-month target price of 
NT$400 is based on an 17.9x 2027E P/E multiple, which is set in line with peers’ PEG&M 
ratio (i.e., P/E vs. forward-year earnings growth and OPM). 
Key downside risks: (1) slower-than-expected ramp-up of the AI server business; (2) 
weaker-than-expected EV total solution performance across EV assembly, design, 
software, and semis; (3) slower-than-expected ramp-up of capacity globally; and (4) 
fiercer-than-expected competition in the consumer electronics EMS business.  
Quanta (2382.TW, Neutral): AI servers ramp-up to drive growth ahead; model transition and PC 
shipment slowdown could weigh on 4Q26

Quanta Sep revenues declined by 23% MoM, which we attributed to AI servers model 
transition, while PC entered quarter-end at a strong MoM of +62% MoM. Looking 
forward, we expect monthly revenues to resume sequential growth in Oct to Dec, riding 
on a reducing impact of AI servers model transition as new racks ramp up, along with 
growing general servers on agentic AI, and ASP increase on better specification and high 
raw material costs. We expect 4Q26E revenues to decline by 4% QoQ, considering the 
high base in 3Q26 and the gradual ramp-up of new AI server racks. As we highlighted in 
Oct (Global Server TAM updates), we expect global AI server racks (powered by NVIDIA 
and AMD) shipment at 63k / 110k / 164k in 2026E-28E along with the specification 
upgrade to drive the ASP; we raised leading US CSPs aggregate capex by 4% / 17% / 
22% in 2026E-28E to US$1,397bn by 2028E, reflecting our positive view on AI spending.

Maintain Neutral on fair valuation.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Quanta 
We are Neutral rated on Quanta. Our 12-month target price of NT$300 is based on a 
10.7x 2027E P/E, which references PC/server peers’ EPS growth vs. P/E multiple 
correlation. We see EPS growth as a major factor for the stock’s performance. Key 
upside/downside risks include: 1) stronger-/weaker-than-expected PC market 
recovery; 2) faster-/slower-than-expected AI server ramp-up; and 3) 
stronger-/weaker-than-expected demand for general servers. 
Wiwynn (6669.TW, Buy): New ASIC and GPU AI server racks in expansion

Wiwynn delivered strong Sep revenues, which were 19% ahead of our estimates, 
reflecting strong general server demand from US leading CSPs, and its major customers’ 
new ASIC AI server ramping up. We expect revenue YoY growth to remain strong at mid 
double-digit through Dec 2026, leading 4Q26 revenues to grow at +60 YoY / +8% QoQ, 
driven by (1) major customers’ new ASIC AI servers ramping up, across air-cooled and 
liquid-cooled, (2) general servers end demand remaining solid on agentic AI, (3) new 
GPU-powered AI server racks entering mass production in 4Q26 across NVIDIA and 
AMD platforms, and (4) potential new customers in ASIC AI servers. As we highlighted in 
Oct (Global Server TAM update), we raised AI servers implied AI chips shipment by 9% / 
19% in 2027 / 28E with ASIC penetration rate increasing to 58% by 2028E, and our US 
internet team forecasts that leading US cloud service providers (CSPs) in aggregate will 
grow their capex budgets by 84%/ 52%/ 13% YoY in 2026-28E, with our US CSP capex 
estimates raised by 4% / 17% / 22% in 2026-28E to US$1,397bn by 2028E, supporting 
our positive view on Wiwynn. Maintain Buy with an unchanged 12m TP of NT$4,000

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Wiwynn 
We are Buy-rated on Wiwynn with a 12-month TP of NT$4,000. Our target price is 
based on 20.3x 2027E P/E, which is derived from PC/server peers’ EPS growth vs. P/E 
multiple correlation. We see EPS growth as a major factor for the stock’s performance. 
Key downside risks: 1) weaker-than-expected AI server demand growth; 2) 
slower-than-expected recovery in general server demand; and 3) more severe 
competition in the server ODM market.  
Wistron (3231.TW, Buy): AI server racks to see strong ramp-up with customer expansion and 
specification upgrades

Wistron’s Sep revenue was up 87% YoY to NT$381bn, or 18% lower than our estimate, 
which we attribute to the impact of shipment timing volatility in higher-ASP AI products. 
We expect sequential revenue growth ahead through Dec 2026, driven by new GPU AI 
server racks ramp-up in 4Q26, which should drive up the ASP , along with strong AI 
spending and expansion of the company’s customer base. The new GPU AI server racks 
have various racks across GPU, CPU, and LPU, supporting ODM growth in coming 
quarters. Sep PC shipment was up 7% MoM but down 32% YoY to 1.5m units, reflecting 
the slow PC demand amid the consumption pull-in under the high memory cost. Despite 
PC shipments growth likely being negative HoH considering the strong pull-in since late 
2025, we expect the strong AI server rack ramp-up to offset the impact, driving 2H26 
revenue up +41% HoH. Maintain Buy with an unchanged TP of NT$291 (12.6x 2027E P/E 
vs. 30.4% NI YoY in 2027-28E).

ASUS (2357.TW, Neutral): AI server racks in expansion along with PC product mix upgrade

3-month revenues preview: We expect sequential revenues decline in 4Q26 at 6% qoq,

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Inventec  
We are Neutral-rated on Inventec with a 12-month target price of NT$61.0. Our target 
price is based on 14.2x 2027E P/E, which is derived from PC/server peers’ EPS growth 
vs. P/E multiple correlation. We see EPS growth as a major factor for the stock’s 
performance. 
Key upside/downside risks: 1) stronger-/weaker-than-expected PC market recovery; 
2) faster-/slower-than-expected AI server ramp-up; and 3) 
stronger-/weaker-than-expected demand for general servers.

### Page 14

(2) broadband, IoT, and game console entering peak season. We are positive on

Maintain Sell on stretched valuation.

Pegatron’s Sep revenue was up 87% YoY / 143% MoM to NT$215bn, or 64% higher than 
our estimate. This reflects a strong ramp-up in AI servers in Sep. Sep PC shipments were 
up c.8% MoM but down 44% YoY to 0.46m, reflecting the weak PC demand under the 
high memory costs. Prior to September, the company suffered from model transition (i.e. 
declining demand for older models while new models were in a slow shipment ramp-up 
phase).

Earnings revision: We factor in Pegatron’s Sep revenue and raise our 2026-28E net 
income by 4% / 6% / 4%, mainly on higher revenues and lower opex ratio. We raise our 
2026-28E revenues by 16% / 28% / 27%, considering the AI server shipment ramp-up 
and higher ASP on better specification. Given the much lower GM of AI server racks 
compared to Pegatron’s other products, our changes in gross profits narrow to 9% / 12% 
/ 15% in 2026E-28E. The opex ratio for 2026-28E is cut by 0.1 ppt/ 0.2 ppt/ 0.1 ppt to 
reflect the improved operational efficiency.

What surprised us 
AirTAC reported 3Q26 results today (October 29th). 3Q OP was in 
line with our estimates, but was 5% higher than BBG consensus. 
OPM was 33.9% in 3Q26, largely in line with our estimates; although 
GM of 49.4% was higher than our estimates by 0.9ppt, it was offset 
by higher opex (NT$1,778mn vs. GSe of NT$1,669mn). Total 
revenue was largely in line with GSe but was 4% above consensus, as 
the company enjoyed solid demand from the electronics industry 
(33% YoY in 3Q26 vs. 20% YoY in 2Q26, which accounted for 28% of 
total 3Q26 revenue). 
What surprised us the most during the results call was the 
company’s solid guidance on industry growth (mentioned low single 
to mid single digit % YoY growth is sustainable for the longer term 
for the China pneumatic market) and the company further 
mentioned that it could outgrow the market by 10ppt YoY in 2027. 
Moreover, the company expects all 
electronics/automotive/machine tools/general 
machinery/textile/packaging to see double digit YoY growth in both 
2026/27, thanks to the China government’s ongoing stimulus 
policies. Management reiterated 2026 revenue guidance in Rmb 
terms to reach 20%+ YoY growth (could reach higher YoY in NT$ 
terms), and maintained 2026 OPM guidance of 34%.  
The company reiterated its utilization rate target for its linear guide 
business to reach 50% by 2026 end (vs. 40%+ in 3Q26). The 
company continues to see peers raise prices for their linear guide 
products, and management thinks the company is well-positioned 
to gain market share with its stable pricing and shorter product lead 
time (less than a month vs. a key competitor at 4 months, see here).  
Going into 4Q26, we expect revenue to reach NT$11,425mn (-1% 
QoQ,+26% YoY), as we expect solid YoY across nearly all end 
markets. Rising raw material cost remains a key risk, while the

### Page 3

company continues to maintain a stable pricing strategy within the China market (which 
accounts for 90%+ of total revenue); thus we expect GM to slightly decline to 48.7% 
(-0.7ppt QoQ and +1.2ppt YoY). We continue to see the company’s ongoing effort in 
improving internal efficiency, which could partially offset the negative impact from rising 
material cost. 
We hold our conservative though optimistic view on the pneumatic industry outlook, as 
we continue to see downside risk in consumer electronics shipments (smartphone, etc) 
and the negative impact from rising raw material cost to persist over the coming 
quarters. We expect the company to continue to gain share in the China pneumatic 
market with its stable pricing strategy as well as its shift towards higher-quality 
pneumatic products.

Investment view 
We reiterate our Buy rating on AirTAC as we expect the company will continue to 
outperform the overall pneumatic market. We are positive on the company’s latest 
comment that the overall China pneumatic market YoY growth could be sustained at low 
single to mid single digit for a longer period, and management further mentioned that 
the company will outperform the market by 10ppt in 2027. This is mainly driven by the 
company’s latest guidance that nearly all end markets could see double digit YoY growth 
in 2027. While the company did mention the risk of decelerating YoY growth for the 
battery industry (which has seen solid YoY growth over the past two years), we expect 
solid growth from smaller segments, including machine tool/general 
machinery/textile/packaging, could offset the risk (we expect the 4 segments to account 
for 24% of total 2027E revenue vs. battery at 13%). 
We believe AirTAC is well positioned to overcome potential industry demand risks with 
its strong B/S and healthy cash flow position, which could further support market share 
gains during industry downturns (historically, AirTAC has accelerated its market share 
gains during industry downcycles, supported by its solid B/S and cash flow 
management). We also expect the company’s ROE to continue to improve in the coming 
years (ROE should improve to 26.2% in 2028E per GSe vs. 16%-17% in 2022-25) 
supporting a high dividend payout ratio (50%-60%+ per year per GSe). 
As a result, we maintain our Buy rating on AirTAC with unchanged 12m TP of NT$1,895.

Earnings and TP revisions 
We revise up our 2027/28E revenue estimates by 1/1% to factor in the 
better-than-expected growth in the China pneumatic market. We revise up our 2026E 
OPM by 0.3ppt to factor in actual 3Q26 results and revise up our 2027/28E OPM by 
0.3/0.1ppt as we continue to expect the company to focus on enhancing internal 
efficiency. We revise down our 2026E EPS estimates by 4% after factoring in a higher tax 
rate in 2H26. 
Also, we introduce our 2029E earnings estimates in this report. 
Maintain our Buy rating and 12m TP of NT$1,895, which is based on unchanged P/E of 
26x on our 2027E EPS, as we believe the company could trade at 0.25x STDV above the 
average PE multiple during the previous pneumatic upcycle.

### Page 6

Investment Thesis - AirTAC
AirTAC is the 2nd-largest pneumatic supplier in mainland China with 28% market share
in 2025, outpacing most local competitors, owing to its low-cost yet high quality
product, and service quality reputation in most key cities in mainland China. We forecast
its market share will reach 30% in 2026E and expect AirTAC to be the structural winner
in a deflationary market, due to its: (1) strong market growth and leadership: AirTAC

continues to expand its market share in mainland China driven by competitive pricing
that is more than 10% lower vs. international peers (with 45%+ GM / 30-35% OPM) and
high demand elasticity among mainland China customers; and (2) superior cost structure
and financial health: with best-in-class cash cost and a solid balance sheet, featuring
positive net cash over FCF since 2023/2024, we believe AirTAC is well-equipped to
weather industry downturns and capitalize on market restructuring.

Overall, we believe AirTAC will see better revenue/profitability in the long term given its
share gainer position in the China pneumatic market and potential to gain share in the
linear guide market (improved demand in the next 1-2 years). This should lead to
strengthened valuation, vs. its recent trading range (trading below its 10-year avg. P/E
multiple). We are Buy rated on AirTAC.
Price target risks and methodology - AirTAC
Valuation methodology: Our 12m TP of NT$1,895 is based on a target P/E of 26.0x on

our 2027E EPS (0.25x STDV above avg of the last pneumatic upcycle).
Key downside risks: (1) Stronger competition from peers; (2) sharper-than-expected
Rmb depreciation, and (3) a longer-than-expected end-market demand downtrend in
mainland China.

on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

HD현대중공업의 3Q26 연결 실적은 매출액 5조 8,602억원(yoy +33%, qoq -7%), 
영업이익 9,161억원(yoy +64%, qoq -12%, OPM 15.6%)으로 시장 컨센서스를 하회할 
전망이다. 동사의 3Q26 조업일수는 여름휴가, 추석연휴 및 중대재해 등 영향으로 
qoq -21% 감소했으며 환율 역시 qoq -84원 감소한 영향을 감안해 전분기 대비 
실적을 보수적으로 추정했다. 그럼에도 해양 부문 Trion FPU발 C/O에 따른 일회성 
환입이 예상됨에 따라 일부 이익 상쇄가 가능할 것으로 예상된다. 한편, 동사의 
임단협 부결로 당분기 추가적인 인건비 부담은 제한적일 것으로 판단되며 잠수함 
화재 사고 관련 비용 역시 4Q 이후 반영될 전망이다.

동사는 급격히 커져가는 AIDC향 전력수요 및 발전용 엔진 수주에 대응하고자 9/10 
총 4GW 규모의 4행정 중속엔진 캐파 증설을 위한 신규 투자를 공시헸다. 동사의 
기존 DC향 4행정 중속엔진 수주 단가를 고려 시 GW당 약 1조원의 매출 창출이 
가능할 전망이며 2행정 및 선박용 4행정 엔진 대비 높은 수익성이 예상되는 바 
생산이 본격화될 28년부터 동사 실적 성장성의 핵심 동인으로 작용할 전망이다.

동사에 대해 투자의견 BUY를 유지하며 업종 밸류에이션 디레이팅 및 시장 프리미엄 
조정에 따른 Target Multipe 변동으로 목표주가를 70만원으로 하향 조정한다. 상선 
시장 수주 경쟁 심화로 업종 밸류 조정이 이루어지고 있으나 동사는 엔진 기반 장기 
성장성으로 Peer 대비 상대적 매력도 확보 및 주가 방어가 가능하다는 판단이다.

## Document 230: [329180] HD현대중공업(329180) 피크아웃 우려를 이겨낼 조선사HD현대중공업(329180) 피크아웃 우려를 이겨낼 조선사피크아웃 우려를 이겨낼 조선사 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[329180] HD현대중공업(329180) 피크아웃 우려를 이겨낼 조선사HD현대중공업(329180) 피크아웃 우려를 이겨낼 조선사피크아웃 우려를 이겨낼 조선사 - 기업.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-29
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

(329180) 
HD 현대중공업 
2026.10.29

AI로보틱스/방산/조선/운송 양승윤 
6139 / syyang0901@eugenefn.com

피크아웃 우려를 이겨낼 조선사 
 투자의견 BUY(유지) 
 목표주가 610,000원(하향) 
 현재주가 285,500원(10/28)

 3Q26 매출액은 5조 7,536억원(+30.2%yoy), 영업이익은 8,992억원
(+61.3%yoy, OPM 15.6%)을 기록. 영업이익 컨센서스 9,792억원 하회.  
 평균 환율이 약 79원 하락하며 영업이익 약 450억원이 줄었고, 조업
일수는 47일로 통상 계절성 대비 4일 추가 감소해 약 500억원의 이
익 감소 효과 발생. 임단협 추가 비용의 약 75%도 3분기에 선반영
(잔여분은 4분기 반영). 일회성 요인을 제외하면  이익 체력은 견조. 
 동사의 조선 부문 매출액은 4조 3,988억원(+44.0%yoy), 영업이익은 
6,463억원(+62.8%yoy, OPM 14.7%)을 기록. 상선 OPM은 14.7%로 
2Q26(17.8%) 대비 하락했으나 대부분 외생변수 영향. 3Q26 대형선 매출 
중 2023년 수주분 비중은 45%, 2024~2025년 수주분은 51%. 고선가 
물량의 매출 비중이 높아지는 2027년까지 수익성 개선 여력은 충분.  
 9월 말 누적 상선 수주는 138.0억달러로 연간 목표의 120%, 엔진기계는 
41.4억달러로 155%를 달성했으나 함정(13%)과 해양(5%)은 부진. LNG선
은 중국 조선소의 저가 수주와 발주 둔화로 선가가 일부 하락. 선주들이 현 
선가를 저점으로 인식하며 발주 협의를 재개하는 분위기. 북미 LNG 프로젝
트 FID가 이어지고 있어 2027년 LNG선 발주 회복 기대. 
 함정 부문은 매출 2,570억원(-14.6%qoq)으로 줄었으나, 수출 비중 확대에 
따른 믹스 개선으로 영업이익 386억원(+114%qoq, OPM 15.0%)을 기록. 
해양플랜트는 매출액 3,799억원(+35.3%yoy), 영업이익 1,265억원(OPM 
33.3%). C/O 180억원을 제외해도 Ruya 공정 본격화와 Trion 고수익 물량
으로 높은 수익성 유지. 2027년 일부 프로젝트 인도로 매출 감소가 예상되
나, LNG선·블록 물량 투입으로 고정비 부담을 완화할 계획. 신규 해상변전
소(OSS) 계약을 앞두고 있으며, 2027년 초 추가 수주 발표도 기대. 
 주목할 부분은 엔진기계. 영업이익 2,299억원(OPM 24.2%)으로 높은 D/F 
엔진 비중을 바탕으로 견조한 수익성 유지. 육상발전용 힘센엔진은 올해   
3GW를 확보하며 2029년까지 계획 캐파 수준의 물량을 이미 채움. 생산 
예상 물량은 2028년 약 1.5GW, 2029년 약 3GW이며, 신규 공장 구축 이
후 연간 생산능력은 약 4GW로 확대 예정. 북미 Hyperscaler와의 직접 협
상이 늘고 있으며, 힘센엔진을 활용한 부유식 발전설비(BMPP)로 FDC 시장
까지 대응 가능. AI 데이터센터 전력 수요가 2028년 이후 새로운 이익 축. 
 미국에서는 Kiewit과 합작법인 설립 계약 체결. 군수지원함 등 지원함 사업
부터 추진하며, 현지 조선소 건설 과정에서 HII에 선박 블록을 공급하는 방
안도 협의 중. 구체적인 투자계획은 2026년 말까지 확정 목표. 국내에서는 
핵추진잠수함 초기 예산이 2027년 반영되며 동사도 사업 참여를 준비 중. 
 동사에 대한 투자의견 BUY, 목표주가 61만원을 제시. 고선가 물량 매출 확
대와 육상발전용 엔진 성장으로 피크아웃을 이겨낼 굴지의 조선사.

Compliance Notice 
당사는 자료 작성일 기준으로 지난 3개월 간 해당종목에 대해서 유가증권 발행에 참여한 적이 없습니다 
당사는 본 자료 발간일을 기준으로 해당종목의 주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다  
당사는 동 자료를 기관투자가 또는 제 3자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다 
조사분석담당자는 자료작성일 현재 동 종목과 관련하여 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다 
동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 조사분석담당자 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다 
동 자료는 당사의 제작물로서 모든 저작권은 당사에게 있습니다 
동 자료는 당사의 동의 없이 어떠한 경우에도 어떠한 형태로든 복제, 배포, 전송, 변형, 대여할 수 없습니다 
동 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료 및 정보로부터 얻어진 것이나, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 어떠
한 경우에도 자료는 고객의 주식투자의 결과에 대한 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙자료로 사용될 수 없습니다

## Document 256: Business Checkpoint_ Looking into the logistics Profitability growth was positive in the third quarter, but logistics costs and supply chain pressures are creating challenges. 2 mi

Source PDF: all report pdf/Business Checkpoint_ Looking into the logistics Profitability growth was positive in the third quarter, but logistics costs and supply chain pressures are creating challenges. 2 min read.pdf
Report source: Bank of America
Date: 2026-10-01
Pages: 8
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

13028181     1

Economy 
Business Checkpoint: Looking into the logistics 
15 October 2026

Key takeaways 
• Small and mid-sized businesses entered Q3 in relatively solid financial condition, with profitability growth up across both 
groups, according to Bank of America business account data. Nearly half of estimated tariff payments had been refunded by 
September, though the share of these refunds relative to revenue has peaked. 
• In Bank of America business payments data, logistics spending growth per client was up four times as much for small than mid-
sized firms. Additionally, distribution and inventory transactions growth per client has outpaced spending since the spring of 
2025. 
• Growing transportation bottlenecks are re-emerging ahead of the holiday season. Trucking capacity has tightened as driver 
availability remains constrained, according to BofA Global Research. This has pushed freight rates higher and driven strong 
transportation sector payroll growth, particularly among mid-sized businesses, according to Bank of America payments data.

Business Checkpoint is a new, regular publication from Bank of America Institute. It provides a real-time view of the financial health, opportunities 
and challenges facing small and mid-sized businesses across the US economy, leveraging the depth and breadth of Bank of America's proprietary 
data. By analyzing activity among both small and mid-sized firms, Business Checkpoint offers timely insights into business profitability, hiring, 
investment and spending trends. Such data is not intended to be reflective or indicative of, and should not be relied upon as, the results of operations, 
financial condition or performance of Bank of America. 
Businesses end Q3 in fair shape 
While profitability growth remained positive for both small and mid-sized firms in the third quarter, small firms outpaced their 
mid-sized counterparts for the second consecutive quarter (Exhibit 2). Still, there’s been a notable reversal in the profitability of 
mid-sized businesses, growing this quarter after declining slightly for the past two.  
The continued rollout of tariff refunds is serving as a tailwind to some firms' cash positions. That is particularly true for small 
firms. According to Bank of America account data, by September approximately 47% of estimated International Emergency 
Economic Powers Act-related tariff payments had been refunded to companies with less than $50 million in annual revenue.  
Exhibit 1: Tariff refunds as a share of income for businesses <$50M 
in annual revenue peaked in June

### Page 2

2 15 October 2026 
  I NSTI TUTE

According to Bank of America business payments data, the percentage point difference in tariff refunds as a share of revenue 
for firms with less than $50 million in annual revenue spiked in June but dropped to nearly zero in September. At the same time, 
the percentage point difference in tariff payments as a share of expenses began rising again relative to the 2023 average 
(Exhibit 1). This suggests that while tariffs continue to affect companies, the refund program has provided temporary relief for 
some businesses.  
Wholesale trade along with transportation and warehousing posted solid profitability growth across business size 
However, the benefits appear uneven. Such refunds have boosted cash flow, but they have not fully insulated firms from broader 
cost pressures.  
Looking across industries, mid-sized construction and wholesale firms had the strongest profitability growth in September 
(Exhibit 3). Notably, transportation and warehousing, as well as wholesale trade were among the few sectors showing positive 
profitability growth for both small and mid-sized firms last month. 
The persistence of logistics, freight and inventory-related cost pressures may help explain why profitability gains have remained 
relatively concentrated in transportation and wholesale trade. However, these sectors also have a higher exposure to rising gas 
prices within their margins (read more on this in the August Small Business Checkpoint). 
Exhibit 3: For both small and mid-sized businesses, profitability growth was positive in transportation and warehousing as well as wholesale trade 
in September 
Profitability growth by sector and business size in September (YoY%, six-month moving average)

Source: Bank of America internal data  
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE

The cost of maintaining supply chains is rising 
In fact, the prices paid component of the September services reading from the Institute for Supply Management indicates still-
building price pressure for firms (Exhibit 4). This marked the highest reading since July 2022.   
Using Bank of America internal data, we identified payments to logistics and freight companies to assess a key part of supply 
chain costs. In June, logistics and freight payments’ growth per client from small businesses grew nearly 30%, though has 
moderated to 12.7% YoY in September (Exhibit 5). Meanwhile, mid-sized firms’ payments growth in this category was up just 
3.2% YoY in September. 
The divergence suggests logistics costs may be disproportionately affecting smaller firms, who are typically less capable of 
absorbing rising costs than larger firms. Larger companies also often benefit from dedicated logistics contracts and greater 
purchasing power, while smaller ones may be more exposed to spot-market transportation costs and fluctuations in freight 
pricing.

BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE

Evidence of more moving parts in companies’ supply chains 
Yet there is increasing evidence that tariffs and geopolitics are adding complexity to small and mid-sized companies’ supply 
chains. 
In Bank of America payments data, average distribution and inventory transaction growth per business client has increased 
steadily since the spring of 2025 despite spending growth in this category falling in September (Exhibit 6). Rising transaction 
growth alongside softer spending growth suggests businesses may be engaging with more suppliers, shipments or inventory 
movements even as overall procurement outlays moderates.  
Furthermore, in May 2026, the Global Supply Chain Pressure Index reached its highest reading since the summer of 2022 and has 
continued to remain elevated into October (Exhibit 7). This further emphasizes that transportation bottlenecks, delays and 
procurement challenges are becoming more common globally. While current readings remain below the extreme levels observed 
during the pandemic, the recent move higher indicates supply chain normalization may be reversing.

per business client has increased steadily since the spring of 2025

The trials of trucking  
As of October 1, BofA Global Research's proprietary bi-weekly Truckload Demand Indicator (see Methodology) remained below 
its historical growth signal (Exhibit 9) and the Truck Capacity Indicator, which reflects shippers' views of available capacity, 
tightened significantly, falling from 39.2 to 28.4 (Exhibit 8). This suggests shippers are seeing fewer trucks available relative to 
demand than in the previous survey. 
According to BofA Global Research, the current trucking upcycle is being driven by constrained supply rather than a significant 
improvement in freight demand. While any positive movement in demand is an important indicator as the industry moves into 
peak shipping season between late September and early December, tighter capacity appears to be the dominant force 
supporting freight rates. 
Exhibit 8: Shippers’ view of available capacity has fallen 
significantly as of October 1, 2026 
BofA Truckload Capacity Indicator by year (bi-weekly)

Source: BofA Global Research  
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE

Exhibit 9: The Demand Indicator has been sub-60 for the past two 
months after 4.5 months above 
BofA Truckload Demand Indicator (bi-weekly)

Source: BofA Global Research  
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE

The reduction in capacity reflects several factors, including stricter commercial driver's license (CDL) and training requirements, 
increased compliance and enforcement measures, and other regulatory changes that have reduced the pool of qualified drivers. 
This has also likely driven some wage inflation within the industry. According to Bank of America payments data, transportation 
and warehousing payroll growth per client has surged since the start of the year, especially for mid-sized businesses (Exhibit 10).  
Having to pay higher wages suggests labor shortages remain a challenge in the sector. Firms may increasingly need to offer 
higher compensation to attract and retain workers as trucking capacity tightens. In turn, such capacity puts upward pressure on 
freight rates, particularly in the spot market where small businesses may be more impacted.

Logistics payments tend to increase in the second half of the year 
September marks a key run-up to the holiday season (read more on signs of holiday spending in the October Consumer 
Checkpoint), with spending on ocean shipping, containers, and other transportation services steadily accelerating ahead of the 
peak shopping period.  
In fact, over the past two years, the month of November has seen ocean shipping/container spending volumes well above their 
2023-2025 monthly average (Exhibit 11). The seasonal pattern likely reflects inventory restocking and holiday-related shipping 
activity. Ocean shipping volumes typically accelerate during the fall months as businesses prepare for year-end demand. 
Exhibit 11: Ocean shipping/container spending volumes were well above their 2023-2025 monthly average in November 
Logistics and freight average payments by category (monthly, indexed, 100 = monthly 2023-2025 average)

Source: Bank of America internal data  
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE 
Distributors and inventory payments’ growth fell ahead of holiday season 
Changes in logistics and freight spending often provide an early signal of inventory trends, as distributors account for a large 
share of supply chain-related spending (Exhibit 12). 
The sharp moderation in distribution and inventory spending growth per client for both small and mid-sized businesses indicates 
that much of the large-scale inventory accumulation seen earlier in 2025 has already occurred (Exhibit 13). In other words, 
businesses appear to be replenishing inventories for seasonal demand rather than significantly expanding stockpiles as they did 
earlier in the year. 
Overall, businesses seem to be entering the holiday season in relatively stable financial condition, supported by tariff refunds 
and positive profitability growth. However, rising logistics costs, tighter trucking capacity and elevated supply chain pressures 
suggest operating conditions are becoming more challenging.

Methodology 
Selected Bank of America transaction data is used to inform the macroeconomic views expressed in this report and should be considered in the 
context of other economic indicators and publicly available information. In certain instances, the data may provide directional and/or predictive 
value. The data used is not comprehensive; it is based on aggregated and anonymized selections of Bank of America data and may reflect a 
degree of selection bias and limitations on the data available. 
Any Business payments data represents aggregate spend from Small Business and/or Business Banking clients with a deposit account or a 
Small Business credit card. Payroll payments data include channels such as ACH (automated clearing house), bill pay, checks and wire. Bank of 
America per Business client data represents activity spending from active Business clients with a deposit account or a Small Business credit 
card and at least one transaction in each month. Small businesses in this report include business clients within Bank of America and are 
generally defined as under $5mm in annual sales revenue. Mid-sized businesses in this report include business clients within Bank of America 
and are generally defined as $5mm-$50mm in annual sales revenue. 
Unless otherwise stated, data is not adjusted for seasonality, processing days or portfolio changes, and may be subject to periodic revisions.  
Revenue tiers are determined by the combination of following factors: 1) stated revenue on small businesses credit applications, 2) actual 
account inflow into Bank of America Deposit Accounts, and 3) third party revenue estimation.  
The alternative hiring indicator consists of payments from Bank of America business clients to business-focused hiring firms which include both 
direct deposits through Automated Clearing House (ACH) and payments via credit and debit cards. 
Supply chain-related spending is comprised of payments to logistics, freight, distribution, inventory, delivery service, and trucking companies. 
These are identified from merchant names and MCC codes. 
BofA Global Research’s trucking indicators are a bi-weekly diffusion-style survey of shipper demand expectations over the next 0–3 months, 
built from responses from surveyed shippers. 
Data regarding merchants who receive payments are identified and classified by the Merchant Categorization Code (MCC) defined by financial 
services companies. The data are mapped using proprietary methods from the MCCs to the North American Industry Classification System 
(NAICS), which is also used by the Census Bureau, in order to classify spending data by subsector. Spending data may also be classified by other 
proprietary methods not using MCCs.  
Additional information about the methodology used to aggregate the data is available upon request.

米国

2026 年 10 月 29 日 全5 頁

FOMC 明確さを待つために様子見 
FOMC はインフレ高止まり、景気下振れ、金融面のリスクに直面

経済調査部 主任研究員 矢作 大祐 
ニューヨークリサーチセンター 研究員 藤原 翼

[要約] 
 2026 年10 月のFOMC では、FF 金利の誘導目標レンジを3.75～4.00％に据え置いた。市
場は事前にFF 金利の据え置きを織り込んでおり、決定後の長期金利、株価、ドルにも
大きな変動は見られなかった。 
 声明文では景気判断を上方修正し、ウォーシュFRB 議長も FOMC 後の記者会見で、個人
消費、設備投資、雇用環境の底堅さを強調した。そして、インフレ率は依然高すぎる
ことも引き続き強調し た。他方、金融環境に関するウォーシュ議長の指摘には変化が
見られた。資本配分の選別が進み、一部資産では信用スプレッドが急拡大するなど、
金融環境には引き締まりの兆候が表れているとした。 
 もっとも、ウォーシュ議長は金融環境の引き締まりの 程度を断定するのは時期尚早と
の見解を示した。ウォーシュ議長は今後のデータによって状況がより「明確」になる
と述べ、フォワードガイダンスの提示には慎重な姿勢を維持しつつも、12月のFOMC で
利上げに踏み切る可能性をにじませたと解釈すべきである。 
 9 月時点のFOMC 参加者の金利見通しと直近のFedWatch は、12 月のFOMC での0.25％pt
追加利上げを示唆している。しかし、実施の可否は予断を許さない。注目点は、期待
インフレ率の上振れ、雇用統計、とりわけ労働参加率の改善、社債利回りなどに表れ
る金融ストレスの 3 点である。FOMC は、利上げによる景気・金融環境の悪化と、据え
置きによるインフレ期待の上振れというジレンマに直面しており、政策判断とともに
丁寧な情報発信が求められる。

### Page 2

1

2 / 5

政策金利の据え置きを決定 
2026 年 10 月 27 日・28 日に開催された FOMC（連邦公開市場委員会）では、政策金利である
FF（フェデラルファンド）レートの誘導目標レンジを 3.75～4.00％に据え置いた。なお、今回
の決定について、クックFRB 理事とクリーブランド連銀のハマック総裁が0.25％pt の利上げを
主張して反対票を投じた。 
今回の決定は、市場にとってサプライズとはならなかった。CME が算出する FedWatch（FF 先
物市場から算出される利上げ・利下げ確率）によると、FOMC の開催直前（10 月 26 日）の時点
で、金利据え置きの確率が約 90％となっていた。前回（2026 年 9 月 15 日・16 日）の FOMC で
0.25％pt の利上げが実施された後、FOMC 参加者は根強いインフレを背景に年内の追加利上げを
主張する一方、利上げの時期に関しては慎重に評価していくとの見解を示していたことから、
市場も金利据え置きを織り込んでいた。 
なお、FOMC 後の記者会見において、ウォーシュ FRB 議長は来年以降における FOMC の開催ス
ケジュールに言及した。具体的には、今後も 1 年あたり合計 8 回の会合を行う一方で、そのう
ち 2027 年は 1 回分、2028 年は 2 回分を金融政策の広範な戦略に特化した会合に充てるとし、
金融政策の決定や声明文の公表は行わないと発表した。

声明文・記者会見：金融環境の変化を指摘する一方、慎重に評価を行う意向 
今回公表された声明文を見ると、冒頭で「委員会は10 対2 の賛成多数で以下の声明の公表を
承認した」と前置きした上 で、「委員会は、連邦準備制度の二重の目標（デュアルマンデート）
を支えるため、FF 金利の目標レンジを3.75～4.00％に維持することを決定した。委員会は、銀
行システムにおける潤沢な準備預金を維持する政策を継続する」と決定内容を示した。 
続いて、景気認識については、「経済活動は力強く拡大している。地政学の動向などにより
不確実性は依然として高いものの、国内支出は底堅く推移している。生産性の伸びは力強く、
設備投資も堅調だ」と、前回の「経済活動は堅調に拡大」から「経済活動は力強く拡大」に上
方修正された。雇用環境については、 「雇用者数の増加は労働力人口の増加に追いついており、
失業率はほとんど変化していない」との表現が据え置かれた。ウォーシュ議長は記者会見で、
2026 年上半期の実質GDP 成長率が上方修正され、第3 四半期も力強い成長が見込まれると指摘
した。さらに、個人消費は堅調で、設備投資は、けん引役となっているハイテク関連だけでは
なく、それ以外の項目にも波及が見られると述べた。他方でウォーシュ議長は、多くのFOMC 参
加者は景気が過熱しているとは評価しておらず、その要因として 、自身の見立てでは生産性が
改善していることに言及した。また、雇用環境については、新規失業保険申請件数が低水準で
推移していると指摘し、極めて良好と評価した。 
物価関連の記述を見ると、 「インフレ率は依然として高水準にある。本日の政策措置は、委
員会が掲げる 2％目標への早期回帰を支援するものである。委員会は物価安定を実現する」と

### Page 3

1

3 / 5

の表現から「インフレ率は依然として高水準にある。より多くの情報が集積されることで、委
員会はインフレ率を目標の 2％に速やかに戻すための適切な政策スタンスを決定できるだろう。
委員会は物価安定を実現する」に変更された。ウォーシュ議長は記者会見で、インフレ率は依
然として高すぎると指摘した。さらに、直近数週間においてエネルギー価格やそれに関連する
価格が再上昇していることに警戒感を示した。もっとも、ウォーシュ議長は FRB の取り組みを
評価する指標として期待インフレ率を挙げ、現時点では正しい方向に進んでいるとの認識を示
した。 
また、今回の記者会見では、金融環境についての質問が目立った。ウォーシュ議長は、一部
の企業や家計において金融環境は引き締まりつつあるとの見解を示し、 市場参加者は資本配分
について以前よりも慎重かつ選別的な姿勢をやや強めていると言及した。足元で注目される AI
関連投資の資金調達を含め、金利は幅広い分野に直接・間接の影響を及ぼしているとの認識を
示した。9 月初め以降、市場参加者が投資対象をより選別するようになり、一部の資産では信
用スプレッドが急拡大し、その他の資産でも、金融環境は大幅ではないものの、幾分引き締ま
っていると指摘した。 
もっとも、ウォーシュ議長は、金融環境は引き締まりつつあるものの、現時点で「引き締ま
った状態にある」と判断するのは時期尚早との見方を示した。その上で、今後得られるデータ
によって状況がより「明確（more clarity ）」になると述べた。 この「明確」という表現は、
様々な情報を把握するという目的で様子見を決定した今回のFOMC とは対照的な表現と捉えるべ
きだろう。とりわけ、ウォーシュ議長はフォワードガイダンスを示すことに慎重な姿勢を続け
ていることを踏まえれば、自身のそうした姿勢と矛盾しない範囲で、12 月の FOMC で利上げに
踏み切る姿勢をにじませたといえるのではないか。

FOMC はインフレ高止まり、景気下振れ、金融面のリスクに直面 
今回のFOMC では利上げをせず、様子見となった。市場でも据え置きが想定されていたことか
ら、予想通りの結果といえる。10 年債金利や主要株価指数、ドル指数などにも大きな変動は見
られなかった。 
直近のCPI、PPI が市場予想を上回るなどインフレ懸念が強まる中でも、様子見となった背景
には、景気の下振れリスクへの警戒感があるだろう。ウォーシュ議長は景気を堅調と表現した
が、経済指標を見ると、9 月の雇用統計の雇用者数や小売売上高に減速感が見られるなど、屋
台骨である個人消費には下振れリスクがある。また、けん引役の設備投資も CoreWeave 問題な
どを背景に、債務の持続可能性が警戒されており、 社債利回りは上昇している 1。金融環境全
体に関して、OFR（米財務省金融リサーチ室） の金融ストレス指数に注目すると、足元で上昇
（ストレスの強まり）傾向が見られる（図表 1 左図）。内訳を見ると、ボラティリティ要因がプ

ラスに転じ、クレジット要因のマイナス幅も縮小している。金融環境にストレスが見られつつ
ある中で、FOMC 参加者は様子見姿勢を強めたと考えられる。 
では、先行きの金融政策はどう考えればよいか。9月のFOMC で公表された、FOMC 参加者のFF
金利見通し（中央値）では、2026 年12 月末時点でFF 金利は4.1％となっている。10 月のFOMC
でFF 金利が据え置かれたことから、FOMC 参加者の予想通りであれば、12 月のFOMC での0.25％
pt 追加利上げが想定される。なお、10 月 29 日時点の FedWatch でも 12 月の FOMC では 0.25％
pt の利上げ確率が高いと予想されている。また、ウォーシュ議長が FOMC 後の記者会見で今後
得られるデータによって状況がより明確になると述べたことについて、利上げの可能性をにじ
ませたと解釈すべきとしたのは前述の通りだ。 
図表 1 金融ストレス指数、期待インフレ率の期間構造

（注）左図の金融ストレス指数は上昇すると、金融ストレスの強まりを示す。 
（出所）OFR、クリーブランド連銀、Haver Analytics より大和総研作成

他方で、実際に 12 月の FOMC で利上げを実施できるか否かは予断を許さない。利上げの可能
性を考える上では、3 つの注目点を取り上げたい。1 つ目の注目点として、インフレ率の高止ま
りリスクに関連して、期待インフレ率に注目したい。ウォーシュ議長はFOMC 後の記者会見で、
現時点で期待インフレ率が正しい方向に向かっていると評価したが、クリーブランド連銀が算
出した期待インフレ率の期間構造を見ると、2026 年 10 月は 2026 年 9 月に比べて上方シフトし
た（図表1 右図）。期待インフレ率の上方シフトがさらに進めば、アンカリング機能が低下して
いるとの懸念から、FOMC が利上げ姿勢を強めると考えられる。なお、11 月分の期待インフレ率
に関しては、11 月10 日に公表が予定されている。 
他方で、2 つ目の注目点として、実体経済の下振れリスクに関連して、11 月6 日や12 月4 日
に公表予定の雇用統計、とりわけ労働参加率が挙げられる 2。労働参加率は 8 月以降上昇に転

じた。労働需給や賃金上昇圧力の緩和だけでなく、消費者の雇用マインドの下支えという意味
でも労働参加率の改善が継続するかが重要となろう。そして、3 つ目の注目点は、金融環境だ。
前述の通り、社債利回りといったクレジット要因の悪化に歯止めがかかるかが焦点となる。 
FOMC は、根強いインフレ圧力に対して利上げで対処すれば景気・金融環境を悪化させかねず、
反対に据え置けばインフレ期待の上振れを招きかねないというジレンマに直面しているといえ
る。こうした状況では、政策判断そのものに加え、市場の利上げ 期待の過度な変動を招かない
よう、FOMC 参加者の丁寧なコミュニケーションも重要となろう。

## Document 325: Is AI Useless_

Source PDF: all report pdf/Is AI Useless_.pdf
Report source: researchmail轉寄
Date: 2026-10-27
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

From:    Yardeni QuickTakes <yardeni-research@ghost.io>
Subject: Is AI Useless?
Date:    15 minutes ago
Is AI Useless?

YARDENI QUICKTAKES
Is AI Useless?
By Ed Yardeni & Elias Griepentrog • 26 Oct 2026
View in browser

I. AI's Toxic Financial Trifecta
Recent AI jitters have been fueled by a potentially toxic trifecta of financial
constraints: elevated borrowing costs, uncertain investment returns, and
concerns about whether sufficient capital exists to finance everyone's
ambitious AI spending plans. Consider the following:
(1) Elevated borrowing costs. The 10-year Treasury yield is hovering
near the upper end of its "old normal" range of 4.0%-5.0% (chart). That
puts it at the highest level since 2007. Overall, credit spreads (the extra

### Page 2

yield paid over equivalent US Treasuries, such as the 10-year note) have
not blown out uniformly across the board. Instead, the market has
bifurcated sharply between the pristine balance sheets of mega-cap tech
companies and the leveraged balance sheets of pure-play
infrastructure/data center borrowers.
Yields on AI debt have not broadly surged relative to Treasuries for the
dominant Big Tech issuers, whose balance sheets continue to be treated
virtually like "proxy sovereigns." However, the credit market is increasingly

imposing credit discipline, demanding significantly higher risk premiums
and spreads from the leveraged second- and third-tier infrastructure
borrowers that are building out the physical plumbing of AI. Increased
concerns about the credit quality of AI borrowers have been reflected in
widening credit default spreads (chart).

### Page 3

(2) Uncertain returns. Gartner estimates that AI data center operators
must generate $8.5 trillion in cumulative revenues through 2030 to cover
their investments and associated financing costs and still achieve a 12%
return on invested capital. Yet Gartner projects no more than $3.6 trillion in
attributable AI revenues over that period. In our view, if the numbers don't
add up, the pace of the AI buildout will slow. That wouldn’t be a calamity.
(3) Increasingly constrained capital. Meta's Q3 earnings report

intensified concerns about capital availability. Despite strong revenue
growth, the company reported negative free cash flow for the first time
since 2012 and announced new non-voting shares to facilitate fundraising,
warning of an "increasingly difficult fundraising environment." Today, Meta
unveiled plans to raise up to $95 billion through equity offerings.
Meanwhile, Broadcom CEO Hock Tan identified capital as potentially the
biggest constraint on AI development. BlackRock CEO Larry Fink

described competition for capital as the fiercest of his career, while Nvidia
CEO Jensen Huang observed that neocloud demand is "off the charts,"
but capital is scarce.
Today, Elon Musk warned that more than 200 specialized AI cloud
providers (i.e., neoclouds) are headed for a wave of "guppy-level death
and consolidation." Musk stated that debt markets have "almost entirely
shut off access to money for any company that doesn't have substantial
revenue independent of AI data centers." In recent weeks, the neocloud

providers’ stock prices have tumbled (chart).

### Page 4

On balance, hyperscalers’ stock prices have held up relatively well (chart).
II. Will AI Pay Off?
Demand for compute nevertheless remains extraordinary. Global
spending on cloud infrastructure services surged 43% y/y to $143 billion in
Q2, its fastest growth in eight years. But how sustainable is that demand
growth? Ultimately, the marginal economic benefits derived from AI must
justify the marginal cost of supplying the necessary compute.
We believe AI is already generating tangible economic benefits, even if

they have yet to become clearly visible in aggregate macroeconomic
statistics. Consider the following:
(1) Anthropic's S-1. Anthropic’s IPO filing today offered five revealing
takeaways: First, revenues soared from $1.2 billion in H1-2025 to $16.3
billion in H1-2026. Second, operating losses narrowed from $2.1 billion to
$693 million, with Q2 marking the company's first quarterly operating profit
of $974 million. Third, revenue per dollar of compute expense surged 5.2-

fold to $2.08 between Q1-2025 and Q2-2026. Fourth, Anthropic held
$131.7 billion in cash and short-term investments as of June, alongside
$3.9 billion in H1 operating cash flow. Fifth, its enterprise footprint
expanded rapidly, with more than 11,900 customers spending at least
$100,000 annually as of September, up from roughly 950 a year earlier.
(2) Corporate America's emerging AI payoffs. An August Janus
Henderson study of 109 companies found that 69% reported some margin

### Page 5

improvement associated with productivity gains and AI-related cost
reductions. Among the examples cited, Shell reported $400 million in
annual savings and a 45% reduction in unplanned downtime; Freeport-
McMoRan increased mill throughput 10% while cutting unit costs by up to
15%; inventories at one Hitachi facility fell 50% and lead times shortened
by 77%; and IBM reported $4.5 billion in AI-related savings in 2025.
Last Thursday, Barclays announced plans to eliminate approximately

5,000 jobs, or 5% of its workforce, as AI and broader technological
modernization improve efficiency. PayPal is targeting $1.5 billion in
annualized savings, partly through AI adoption.
(3) Corporate profit margins. Corporate America is becoming more
profitable. The forward profit margin of the S&P 500 excluding its
Information Technology sector has been climbing this year, reaching
12.9% in October (chart). Excluding just the Semiconductors industry, the

forward profit margin rose to a record 14.4%. The forward profit margin in
Semiconductors rose to a record 51.8% in the week of October 23. But
most of the industry’s margin expansion since 2024 is attributable to
capacity constraints pushing prices higher.
A closer look reveals encouraging trends in industries where AI
applications could be particularly beneficial. Forward profit margins have
expanded in numerous financial industries where AI offers significant

opportunities for automation (chart).

### Page 6

Health Care stands to benefit from AI applications that can streamline
clinical workflows and administrative processes (chart).
(4) Bottom line. We aren't yet seeing significant AI-driven profit margin
expansion across the economy, but anecdotal evidence of AI-related
productivity gains is mounting. We expect AI adoption to become
increasingly widespread across industries, eventually delivering
substantial productivity growth and boosting corporate profit margins. AI's

economic payoff may not yet be obvious in aggregate statistics, but that
doesn't mean it isn't already materializing.

leave comments or questions at the end of our QuickTakes, and it

### Page 2

the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.
2
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
BULLETIN
The six-month annualised growth rate in the Westpac–
Melbourne Institute Leading Index, which indicates the
likely pace of economic activity relative to trend three to
nine months into the future, held at –0.03% in September,
unchanged from August.

The Leading Index continues to show moderately improved
momentum compared to the middle of the year, with the
latest reads pointing to growth holding at or slightly below
trend heading into late 2026/early 2027. Growth appears
to be stabilising at a slow pace rather than stalling. That
said, both the latest fuel prices and the RBA’s September
interest rate increase are still working their way through to
the economy with impacts likely to take some of the gloss off

growth near term. Overall, Westpac expects annual growth
to come in at 1.5% for calendar 2026, a below-trend result.
The Leading Index growth rate has firmed slightly over the
last six months, rising from –0.16% back in March to –0.03%
in September. The component detail shows mixed moves over
the period. Four components have contributed more positively
with reduced drags from the Westpac-Melbourne Institute
Unemployment Expectations Index (adding +0.23ppts to

the Index growth rate); the Westpac-Melbourne Institute
Consumer Expectations Index (adding another +0.15ppts);
and the S&P/ASX200 (+0.15ppts) combining with a modest
lift in growth in US industrial production (adding +0.12ppts).
These positives have been partially offset by modest drags
from four previously stable components: dwelling approvals
(–0.34ppts); monthly hours worked (–0.08ppts); commodity
prices, measured in AUD terms (–0.07ppts) and the yield

spread (–0.05ppts).
Overall, the component mix speaks to a lack of direction.
Indeed, despite some large shocks over the last year, the
Leading Index growth rate has been relatively contained since
early 2025, holding in a narrow –0.3% to +0.5% range. This
compares to history which, outside of the COVID and GFC
shocks, has seen growth rates move between a much wider
–1.9% to +1.6% range. Of course, the apparent stability,
which is evident across all components of the index, may not

last but for now it suggests the economy has been stuck in a
bit of a holding pattern over the last two years.
Leading Index still tracking a touch below trend
The Westpac-Melbourne Institute Leading Index of
Economic Activity is designed to assist in identifying
turning points in the economy. The index combines
variables that reflect different aspects of the economy
into a single index that generally produces a more reliable
cyclical indicator than any single component taken

individually. The Leading Index of Economic Activity
provides advance information on the state of the economy
and gives early warnings of cyclical turning points.
Matthew Hassan
Head of Australian Macro-Forecasting

the monthly Westpac-Melbourne Institute Leading Index. We will
continue to publish regular bulletin updates through our westpaciq.
com.au website.

## Document 349: NZドル相場の行方を左右する総選挙とRBNZの政策運営 ～インフレ圧力は根強い一方、政権交代に伴う政策枠組みの変更が不透明要因に～

Source PDF: all report pdf/NZドル相場の行方を左右する総選挙とRBNZの政策運営 ～インフレ圧力は根強い一方、政権交代に伴う政策枠組みの変更が不透明要因に～.pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Asia Trends

2026 年10 月 22 日

1/3 
NZ ドル相場の行方を左右する総選挙と RBNZ の政策運営 
～インフレ圧力は根強い一方、政権交代に伴う政策枠組みの変更が不透明要因に～ 
 経済調査部 主席エコノミスト  西濵 徹 
（℡：050-5474-7495）

（要旨） 
○ ニュージーランドでは、インフレ高進を背景に RBNZ が９月に２会合連続の利上げを実施し
たものの、11 月７日の総選挙を巡る不透明感がＮＺドル相場の重しとなってきた。 新興政党
のオポチュニティ党が連立協議のカギを握る可能性が高まっているうえ、仮に左派・労働党
を中心とする政権への交代が実現すれば、RBNZ の政策目標に雇用の最大化が再び加えられる
と見込まれる。そのため、追加利上げのハードルが高まる可能性が意識されている。 
○ ７-９月のインフレ率は前年比＋3.7％と鈍化し、市場予想も下回ったものの、引き続き目標
レンジを上回っている。エネルギーや食料品を中心にインフレ圧力が後退する一方、サービ
ス物価には上昇圧力が広がる兆しがうかがえる。さらに、原油価格の高止まりや干ばつによ
る食料インフレの懸念も残る。金融市場では早期利上げ観測がくすぶるものの、今回の物価
統計を受けて見方が修正される可能性もあり、先行きのＮＺドル相場は総選挙の結果と、そ
の後のRBNZ の政策枠組みに関する議論に左右される展開が予想される。

【RBNZは追加利上げ実施も、総選挙を巡る不透明感がNZドル相場の重しとなる展開が続いた】 
ニュージーランドでは、RBNZ（ニュージーランド準備銀行）が９月の定例理事会において２会合
連続の利上げを決定した （注１） 。 背景には、2025 年以降のインフレ率が加速してRBNZ の定める目
標レンジ（１～３％）の上限を上回る伸びで推移していることがある。さらに、イラン情勢の悪化
による原油価格の上昇がエネルギーインフレを招き、４-６月のインフレ率は前年比＋4.1％と2023
年 10-12 月以来の高い伸びとなった。こうした事情は、RBNZ の断続的な利上げ実施を後押ししたと
考えられる。 
一方、通貨ＮＺドル相場は上値の重い展開をみせてきた。同国では、11 月７日に議会（代議院：
一院制 （総議席数120） ） の総選挙の実施が予定されており、選挙結果を巡る不透明感が影響してい
るとみられる。世論調査によれば、右派の国民党、ないし左派の労働党を中心とする連立政権がい
ずれも多数派を形成できず、新興政党・オポチュニティ党がキャスティングボートを握る可能性が

本資料は情報提供を目的として作成されたものであり、投資勧誘を目的としたものではありません。作成時点で、第一ライフ資産運用

3/3 
する食料品価格の上昇ペースが鈍化しており、 生活必需品を中心にインフレ圧力は後退した。一方
で、食料品やエネルギーを除いたコアインフレ率は前年比＋2.9％と前期（同＋2.6％）から伸びが
加速したものの、 引き続き目標レンジ内で推移している。 とはいえ、 前期比は＋1.1％と前期 （同＋
0.4％） から上昇ペースが加速しており、 財価格は比較的落ち着いた動きをみせる一方、 サービス物
価で幅広く上昇圧力が強まる動きが確認されるなど、 インフレ圧力が徐々に広がりをみせる兆しも
うかがえる。 
足元では、 イラン情勢を巡る不透明感を理由に原油価格は高止まりしており、 先行きもエネルギ
ーインフレが長引く可能性が高まっている。さらに、エルニーニョ現象の発生を受けて、同国では
東部や北部を中心に、乾燥・干ばつによる農業生産への悪影響が懸念されており、食料インフレや
景気の下押しが懸念される状況にある。 
総選挙においてはインフレも主要な争
点となっており、早期のインフレ抑制に
向けて、RBNZ が前倒しで利上げに動くと
の見方も金融市場に出ており、このとこ
ろの ＮＺドル相場が持ち直 す一因とな
ってきた可能性がある （図３） 。今回の物
価統計が市場予想を下回ったこと を受
けて、 早期利上げ観測が修正される可能
性もある。先行きのＮＺドル相場の行方
を巡っては、総選挙の結果と、その後の
RBNZ の政策枠組みに関する議論に左右
される可能性にも注意が必要と考えられる。

WESTPAC ECONOMICS  
SEPTEMBER CPI PREVIEW
Setting the pace for the run into year end
19 October 2026
Key points
• September CPI to rise 0.62%mth 
with the annual pace lifting to 4.1%yr 
from 4.0%yr. The seasonally adjusted 
CPI lifts 0.5%mth, a step down from 
the 0.7% increase in August.  
• September Trimmed Mean (TM) is 
set to lift 0.26%mth holding the 
annual pace steady at 3.6%yr. 
• As we noted in our review of the 
August CPI, our September quarter 
CPI estimate is unchanged at 1.3%qtr 
while the TM remained 0.9%qtr.  
• Auto fuel surged ahead of our 
expectations so along with signs of 
increase pass-through we lifted our 
December quarter CPI to 1.3%qtr/3.9%yr 
and the TM estimate to 0.9%qtr/3.5%yr. 
• This is a 0.8ppt increase to the CPI 
estimate, and a 0.2ppt increase to the 
TM estimate, highlighting inflation 
momentum meaningfully above 
the RBA’s current expectations.

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
BULLETIN
August inflation came in soft...
Inflation moderated in August following July’s strong 
outcome. The CPI rose 0.4% in the month, in line with our 
expectation but slightly below market consensus of 0.5%, 
taking the annual rate to 4.0%. On a seasonally adjusted 
basis, the CPI increased 0.7%mth, underscoring the impact of 
seasonal discounting in August and the start of the financial 
year repricing in July.
More closely watched are the measures of underlying inflation 
including the Trimmed Mean (TM) and market services. 
The TM rose 0.2% in August, in line with our expectations 
but again softer than the market, leaving the annual pace 
unchanged at 3.6% for a third consecutive month. Meanwhile, 
market services excluding volatiles & holiday travel increased 
0.2%mth/4.1%yr. Notable strength (three-month annualised, 
seasonally adjusted) was seen in sports participation (10.9%), 
clothing repair (7.0%), restaurant meals (6.8%) and other 
household services (5.2%). For full analysis of the August CPI, 
see “August CPI – Came in on our expectations ”.
The expenditure-weighted share of the CPI basket 
experiencing positive price shocks eased to 11% in August 
from 15% in July, while the share experiencing negative price 
shocks declined from 17% to just below 13%. The majority of 
the positive shocks were concentrated in consumer durables 
On a three-month moving average basis, the net momentum 
share (positive minus negative) rose to –4.5% from –7.7% 
in July and a trough of –15.8% in May, suggesting the 
disinflationary impulse may be nearing exhaustion. 
... with September due for a moderate pick-up
Looking ahead to September, early indicators painted a 
mixed picture. The NAB business survey showed retail price 
growth slowing to 0.9%qtr from 1.1%qtr in August, with 
labour cost growth easing to 1.7%qtr from 1.8%qtr, while 
purchase cost growth edged up to 2.4%qtr from 2.3%qtr. 
Nevertheless, all measures remain well above their long-run 
averages while the gap between purchase costs and final 
prices continues to widen, indicating businesses are facing 
margin compression (see here for further detail). Meanwhile, 
consumers’ year-ahead inflation expectations held steady 
at 4.9% in September (easing slightly at 2dp) but surged to 
5.3% in October likely reflecting higher fuel prices, with retail 
petrol and diesel averaging $2.20/l (+8.2%mth) and $2.68/l 
(+7.7%mth). We do believe the auto fuel prices are likely to see 
a near term peak in October.  Meanwhile, global shipping costs 
have also surged recently amid renewed Middle East tensions.
Sep quarter unrevised but Dec upgraded significantly
Justin Smirk, Senior Economist
Neha Sharma, Economist

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
BULLETIN
The September CPI is expected to rise 0.6% (0.62% to 2dp)
with the annual pace lifting to 4.1%yr. Our seasonally adjusted
estimate increases 0.5% (0.48% to 2dp). For the monthly
TM, we expect a 0.3% (0.26% to 2dp) lift, holding the annual

pace steady at 3.6%. On a 3m/3m basis, the TM is tracking
a 1.0% gain in September (noting that while this measure is
conceptually different to the quarterly TM it still serves as a
useful guide). For market services excluding volatiles & holiday
travel we have pencilled in another 0.2% rise, slowing the
annual pace slightly to 4.0% (a nine-month low).
The largest contributors are recreation & culture (+0.27ppt),
driven wholly by holiday travel & accommodation. Transport

(+0.23ppt) makes a solid contribution, with gains in fuel
(+0.26ppt) partly offset by motor vehicles (–0.02ppt). Housing
also contributes (+0.13ppt), with rents and new dwellings
each adding 0.02ppt while property rates add 0.08ppt.
The key detractors in the month are food & non-alcoholic
beverages (–0.08ppt) and garments (–0.04ppt).
Key details for the month of September
Food & non-alcoholic beverages are expected to fall
0.5%mth, easing the annual pace to 2.8%yr. The weakness is

concentrated in a 3.7%mth decline in fruit & vegetables, while
non-alcoholic beverages are also expected to fall 1.5%mth.
Meals out & takeaway is forecast to rise 0.1%mth, easing the
annual rate to 3.9%.
Housing is expected to rise 0.6% in September, slowing the
annual pace to 5.5%yr. Component-wise:
• Rents are expected to rise 0.3%mth and 3.7%yr. CPI rent
inflation is nearing its peak, consistent with the moderation
in asking rent growth. We expect rent inflation to ease to

3.6%yr by end-2026 and 2.5%yr by end-2027.
• New dwelling prices, which capture only the building
component of construction costs, are expected to rise
0.2%mth and 4.9%yr. Ongoing weakness in established
housing markets would be increasing builders’ difficulty in
passing through higher costs to new builds. As a result, we
expect new dwelling inflation to moderate to 4.5%yr by
end-2026 and 2.1%yr by end-2027.
Transport inflation is expected to rise 2.0%mth in September,

lifting the annual pace to 7.7%yr reflecting an 8.3%mth
increase in fuel prices following higher oil prices through the
month. Motor vehicle prices and spare parts are expected to
fall 0.7%mth and 1.3%mth, respectively. In NSW, a one-year
vehicle registration discount between $80-100 came into
effect from 1 September, which is likely to weigh on the other
services in respect of motor vehicles component.
Recreation & culture is expected to rise 2.1%mth driven by a

4.4%mth increase in holiday travel, partly offset by a 0.4%
decline in audio, visual & computing equipment & services and
a 1.3% fall in sporting services.
